Proof `67e62d59c9cecbaf7235ce8bcf2ab807369f0a6235196aa8dbc321c50fb5b9ec`, review `45a7af0785d3efce07c0dba8bbd3c12114494a53b03d971bd76d99255cdde4ae`, constructs an actual semialgebraic inductive certificate for each rational-residue small-loss family already known to be nonattained. The two old rational normals are scaled to integer rows, giving rational monomial potentials. Seven auxiliary variables encode algebraic loss/power-sum constraints and a strict flag; the proof checks the updates at **every** auxiliary state satisfying those inequalities, without assuming it came from a word. A low-pair region is absorbing. Positive denominator margins, the Cauchy update and multiplicative potential inequalities establish induction under both append families. Existential projection supplies an inductive base-state set.

The old corner-uniform estimates justify a terminating search for rational intervals and constants by RCF alone. No logarithmic feasibility oracle is assumed. The certificate excludes every nonordinary point above its rational pair floor at which both normal monomial potentials equal one, including sufficiently small pure Poisson kernels at that rational residue. A supplied finite cover of an entire faithfully compiled target fibre by such rejection regions can be checked by RCF and rejected by intersecting the corresponding invariants. The auxiliary dimension is fixed, but integer monomial exponents and resulting formula degrees vary with the residue. Thus the old necessary unbounded-degree result is respected. ### Positive prefixes, joint hulls and nondefinability

The accepted prefix corollary, proof `bcde94be75ef70340371913a631e42e6d109de74b654fb8079001eb186bbb96f`, review `ac5a0187d82c232b07d9cc26cc848a103a6b650d0729d03d27974d99fda834be`, extends the old forced-boundary mechanism to any fixed positive closure prefix. A transcendental-residue contact forbids a separator in this class; reduced rational residue b/q forces a defining atom of slot degree at least q. The product-hull corollary, proof `4237815f84b50c7674d9a2b3b87a62a1fb6030d1d5cda2bad924898977158f72`, review `256e0d4d75c68c1b3bf42c0a38d8e94a2d8ed9116afc28326364b0ab5e0caa9b`, defines H as the set-theoretic intersection of all one-slot semialgebraic inductive supersets with arbitrary real coefficients. A definition of H would project to an infinite countable definable residue set, contradicting o-minimality. No common residue cutoff or decision at algebraic irrational residues is needed. [Wilkie's primary exposition](https://eprints.maths.manchester.ac.uk/1745/1/Bourbaki_Wilkie.pdf) supplies the parameter-inclusive definition and o-minimality of the real exponential field.

This nondefinability is **not** asserted for the actual source image S, does not identify H with S, and does not exhibit a negative algebraic input lacking a certificate. Coincident rates are included without an exceptional stratum. Two is a sufficient bound, not a one-site impossibility or minimal-feature theorem. ### Prospective practical release gates

All empirical/statistical interpretation remains CONVERGENCE_UNRESOLVED. No further chain batch is silently authorized by this record. These are practical screens, not universal sufficiency guarantees. Probability precision is stricter: Bernoulli MCSE 0.01 can require 2,500 effective draws; a normal-approximation 95% Monte Carlo half-width 0.01 can require 9,604. Nonstationarity prevents a certified runtime or accuracy promise.

The pinned ordinary no-date engine initializes theta/root tau from prior means; no documented explicit continuous-start override was found, and resume restores RNG/state. ### P1: accepted route boundaries and a conditional positive measurement theorem

[Trace-localization component33](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/635139c505ac8ba72261e2f468bbd51fac3fce82/research/2026-10-16-g6-gibbs-certification/trace-localization-pilot-boundary-33/README.md) instantiates standard packing/Fano learning bounds: a classical trace-accurate pilot, or honest confidence cell of diameter at most 2 tau, requires Omega_k(binom(N,k)/tau^2) copies in the stated diagonal family. Its domain is fixed k>=3, 0<tau<=1/(256*9^k), sufficiently large D=binom(N,k), and beta>=8*3^k tau/sqrt(D); fixed beta>0 is eventually admitted. All physical and source-copy costs count. This is a learning/pilot restriction, not an equality-testing lower bound or an exclusion of decision-specific or quantum-output localization. Separate assistant hand/source review; no execution claimed.

[Full local-mean component34](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/9148fcf7184ac9c7565e32ebb95b4fa002f932e1/research/2026-10-16-g6-gibbs-certification/full-local-mean-signal-boundary-34/README.md) supplies, at fixed k>=5 and beta>0 and every sufficiently large physical N, an admitted pair with full trace norm at least 1/4 but optimal pooled covariance-normalized linear-mean signal over ALL weight-at-most-k Pauli features O_beta(1/N). Rare-sector inverse covariance and every auxiliary spin are accounted for. A parity statistic distinguishes the pair easily: full measurement-outcome information is not excluded. This extends the earlier single-score boundary to the full local linear span, not to every statistic or unrestricted testing. Separate hand review and separately authored exact controls supplement the proof; numerical sector diagnostics are explicitly noncertifying.

The [fixed candidate-energy outcome countercheck](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/37c24355314ad28034ab32711c74c8cb2f6300b5/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0358z/p1-energy-outcomes/README.md) gives a constant-separated one-local product pair requiring Omega_beta(N) total copies under the two specified product-basis, classical-output measurement restriction, despite an easy transverse measurement. It is not a bound against arbitrary collective measurements or retained quantum outputs. Hand review, no numerical execution.

In contrast, the [conditional two-energy work test](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/a68948b837963af9c90efb0f0a8eb3d21bb68fe4/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0419z/p1-modular-work/README.md) uses calibrated spectral instruments for the TWO ACTUAL source Hamiltonians. At common beta, full trace gap epsilon and dimensionless readout error zeta<=epsilon/8, its range test uses 2m final copies, m=1+ceil[16 log(1/delta)/(3 epsilon^2)], with zero equality rejection and alternative acceptance at most delta. Coarse bins require Lüders instruments, not merely nearby reported energy numbers. Instrument acquisition, program copies and history-conditioned implementation error remain separately charged; normalized diamond distance is half the diamond norm. This is a conditional positive measurement theorem, not a copy-only P1 improvement. Nussbaum–Szkola laws, Tasaki–Crooks and Fuchs–van de Graaf are established ingredients, not newly discovered connections.

The [acquisition assessment](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/c6981fd51259bbe8e252f41142e41e4ff817eed1/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0419z/p1-modular-work/acquisition/README.md) imports O'Donnell–Wright's established spectrum-testing bound for globally unitary-invariant effective decisions only. On its admitted one-local pair, T>=sqrt(log(13/9))*2^N/a^2 is necessary in that restricted architecture. Locality-aware decisions and Schur transforms retaining usable quantum representation registers are not ruled out. Both conditional-work and acquisition packets have separate scoped hand/source reviews, without execution or a new unrestricted lower bound.

### P1: adequate nonlinear witnesses, with a slower known acquisition bound

The [midpoint Fourier packet](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/a91f02657b622b364a6cc366aac67d4883c77cf0/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0509z/p1-fourier-adequacy/README.md) constructs a source-independent family of at most (2+32 beta M/epsilon)^(2M+1) nonlinear witnesses, M the number of allowed nonidentity Pauli words. Their norm is O(1+log(1+beta M/epsilon)); equality gives nonnegative midpoint loss for every witness, and full trace separation at least epsilon gives loss at most -3epsilon^2/16 for some witness. The weighted-norm Fourier/Sylvester argument avoids inverse minimum eigenvalues. It does not simulate the conditional work law or supply a complete affinity estimator.

Applying established collective Threshold Decision yields O(B^4 epsilon^-4[log L+log(1/delta)]) total copies, hence O(M log^5 M) at fixed beta, locality and accuracy. This is WORSE than the accepted [component13 O(M log M) upper](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/809dcb6dcef78da700fddd888e9177bcff098b0f/research/2026-10-16-g6-gibbs-certification/COLLECTIVE-CERTIFICATION-UPPER-13.md). Adequacy and equality-side second moments alone do not pay adaptive selection or alternative-side aggregation costs. The [stronger lower32](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/9b7bb4034a136542a4d2c06afce84c64d1a50ce3/research/2026-10-16-g6-gibbs-certification/latent-light-fraction-32/README.md), broader-domain lower29 and all earlier valid bounds remain unchanged.

The scoped review accepts the hand argument, using Lu–Fang's variational formulation, classical Fourier identities and the existing threshold theorem. Effective finite implementation requires computable beta/tolerances with certified approximations; an arbitrary known real beta supports the ideal mathematical statement, not an unspecified effective input model. Author exact finite identities and noncertifying numerical diagnostics are supplementary. No efficient runtime, historical novelty or full P1 closure follows.

### P5: contour and Schur route guards, preserving the full response problem

The [KMS-contour packet](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/db7b535691614f1706c9fefc42873d2aaa2f1ee1/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0358z/p5-contour/README.md) supersedes its earlier preliminary status at its reviewed scope. Its grouped modular remainder vanishes at Gibbs input but becomes nonzero immediately under the actual admitted same-H, six-channel, infinite-filter two-site evolution. Automatic repeated equilibrium cancellation therefore fails. Forty finite exact checks and same-script replay supplement the hand/source review; matrix checks do not prove bath admission or the analytic strip argument.

The [disconnected Schur diagnostic](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/914b91f1c1ccdb669fc7dae5bd920f271544eebc/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0419z/p5-schur-return/README.md) separates a signed resolvent return bounded by two from an all-fast-input absolute-return integral growing at least as sqrt(N). Positivity is proved for the specified maximally mixed trajectory, not for the whole Redfield semigroup. Actual local injection is zero, so the actual two-leg memory kernel vanishes. This is not a P5 counterexample. Thirty-one exact finite checks and unchanged-checker replay accompany hand review.

The [connected Schur calculation](https://github.com/Sodelin/Research-Commons/blob/a6c883330d9835a2a765a09c5f63848459105724/research/2026-10-18-dot-new-attacks-0454z/p5-connected-memory/README.md) includes the full filter and proves nonzero first-order actual injected-and-returned memory in boundary coupling. Exterior thermal drift supplies a return without a second boundary interaction. This lies inside the already controlled commuting branch and rejects automatic two-interaction counting, not the comparison theorem. Its auxiliary bound uses frozen propagation and does not estimate the renewed actual compressed evolution. Twenty-six finite exact checks and same-script replay accompany scoped hand/source review. Rooted labelled Pauli coefficient incidence, not the extensive Hamiltonian norm, controls the auxiliary influence bound.

These packets preserve signed-energy25 and all earlier accepted results. Moving projections and other subsequent local stopping notes remain unreviewed, without an accepted integrated estimate. Signed connected repeated propagation, outgoing observable control and a volume/time-uniform stability margin remain unresolved.
